# Resume Import Ledger

[[Resume Bank]] · [[Resume Catalogue.base]] · [[Source Map]]

Approved source collection: `D:\HDD F\downloads\CV\Resumes`. This snapshot accounts for all **1304 files** found in a bounded scan of 1,830 directories. Empty source folders were not recreated.

## Accounting

| Decision | Source files |
|---|---|
| converted-to-markdown | 323 |
| copied-original-and-indexed | 479 |
| exact-duplicate | 66 |
| excluded | 436 |

527 source-version notes represent matching format pairs and exact duplicate families. 479 distinct original documents were copied individually (433 PDFs, 45 Word documents and one image), about 76.1 MB. No source was modified, renamed or removed.

## Interpretation limits

- The default resume view contains 372 Neel-labelled resume records. A matching name is not verification of dates, experience or metrics.
- Other names, anonymous examples, cover letters, references, job descriptions and supporting material remain separated.
- Source experience labels and modification dates are retained as provenance, not accepted as verified facts or a current-master designation.
- LaTeX was converted without executing or compiling it. Exact LaTeX source is folded into its note. PDF and Word text views do not reproduce page layout; preserved originals remain the formatting authority.
- Existing PDF/Word files were copied byte-for-byte, not rewritten or certified as ready to submit. No OCR was performed for the one image reference.
- Generated previews, compiler outputs, temporary/lock files and the stale generated index were excluded. The historical Apple job CSV is outside this resume bank; its existing text summary is imported.
- The manifest under `.copilot/resume-bank` records source and attachment hashes. No abandoned importer or project code was executed.
- This is a snapshot, not a background sync. No tailoring, application, upload, message or submission was performed.

## Verification snapshot

Final checks on 2026-10-14: all 1,304 source hashes are unchanged; all 479 copied originals match their source hashes; all 527 source-version notes have valid generated properties; internal link checks passed. The live Obsidian index reports all 527 records, including all 372 Neel-labelled resume records. The bank contains 530 Markdown notes, one Base, 433 PDFs, 45 Word documents and one image, totaling about 84.4 MB.

Core Climb notes and Company Tracker hashes are unchanged. Existing bookmark groups and property types were preserved; only the Resume Bank shortcut and its styles/types were added. The ribbon remains hidden and Web Browser still uses Google. No external source, application record or submission was changed.
